PWC_ai_multirole_aircraft = {
    allowed = { always = yes }
    enable = {
        is_ai = yes
        pwc_st_is_city_country = yes
    }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = {
        type = equipment_variant_production_factor
        id = pwc_platform_fighter
        value = -100
    }
    ai_strategy = {
        type = equipment_variant_production_factor
        id = pwc_platform_bomber
        value = -100
    }
}
PWC_ai_state_air_defense_1_base = {
    allowed = { always = yes }
    enable = {
        is_ai = yes
        pwc_st_is_city_country = yes
        owns_state = 1
        has_full_control_of_state = 1
        num_of_civilian_factories > 1
        num_of_military_factories > 7
        1 = {
            OR = {
                industrial_complex > 0
                arms_factory > 0
            }
            anti_air_building < 1
            NOT = { days_since_last_strategic_bombing < 30 }
        }
    }
    abort_when_not_enabled = yes
    ai_strategy = {
        type = build_building
        id = anti_air_building
        target = 1
        value = 10
    }
}
